Putting all the bounds together, we get: 
\begin{align*}
    \eqref{eq: sum of fast contracted markovian sum 2}\leq & \beta \underbrace{ \left \| U \right\|_{\text{op}} \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm L)}  \left (  c^{-1} (L_{v_g} + \mathrm{M}_{\mathcal S} \mathrm{M}_\xi L_{K} |\Xi|)  \mathrm{M}_f + \mathrm{M}_\xi\left (  C_A\left ( c^{-1}C_J + \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm L)} ^{-1}\right )  + 1  \right )  \right ) }_{\triangleq B_1}\\
    & + \beta^2  \underbrace{\sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm L)} \left \| U \right\|_{\text{op}}L_{v_g}  \mathrm{M}_g }_{\triangleq B_2}+ \alpha \beta \underbrace{\sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm L)} \left ( C_J\left \| U \right\|_{\text{op}} C_A\mathrm{M}_\xi +  \left \| U \right\|_{\text{op}}L_{v_g}  \mathrm{M}_f \right ) }_{\triangleq B_3}  \\
    & + {\beta^2  \over \alpha } \underbrace{\sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm L)} \left \| U \right\|_{\text{op}} \mathrm{M}_g c^{-1}(L_{v_g} + \mathrm{M}_{\mathcal S} \mathrm{M}_\xi L_{K} |\Xi|)}_{\triangleq B_4}
\end{align*}
We merge the major two bounds together to get: 
\begin{align*}
\eqref{eq: sum of fast contracted markovian sum 1} +\eqref{eq: sum of fast contracted markovian sum 2} \leq &  \alpha A_1 +\beta (A_2 + B_1)+ \alpha ^2 A_3+ \beta^2 B_2 + \alpha \beta (A_4 + B_3) +{\beta ^2 \over \alpha} B_4
\end{align*}
ending the first part of the proof. Now we start with the second part to bound \eqref{eq:slow-bias-term-3}. We start by the first term: 
\begin{align}
    \alpha \left \| \sum_{j=0}^k  (I - \beta J_{yy}^{\alpha, \beta})^{k-j} X\mathbb{E} \left [ \psi_j \right ] \right\|
    \label{eq: poisson sum contraction cross}
\end{align}
as we have discussed above about rewriting the inside terms with the solutions of the Poisson equation, we set $v_f(x_s,y_s, \xi_{s+2}) = v_ s$ and $v_f(x_{s+1},y_{s+1}, \xi_{s+2}) = v_{s+1}$ we have:

\begin{align*}
    \alpha \left \| \sum_{s=0}^k (I- \beta  J_{yy}^{\alpha, \beta})^{k-s} X (v_s - v_{s+1}) \right\|\leq & \alpha \sum_{s=0}^k \left \| I- \beta J_{yy}^{\alpha, \beta} \right\|^{k-s} \left \| X \right\| \left \| v_s - v_{s+1} \right\|\\
    \leq & \alpha\sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)}\left \| X \right\| \sum_{s=0}^k e^{-d\beta (k-s)}\left \| v_s - v_{s+1} \right\|\\
    \leq & \alpha\sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)}L_{v_f}\left \| X \right\| (\alpha \mathrm{M}_f + \beta \mathrm{M}_g)  ( 1 + {1 \over d\beta })\\
    \leq & \beta R_{syl}   \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} L_{v_f}(\alpha \mathrm{M}_f + \beta \mathrm{M}_g)  ( 1 + {1 \over d\beta })\\
    = & \alpha \beta R_{syl}   \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} L_{v_f}  \mathrm{M}_f  + \beta^2 R_{syl}   \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} L_{v_f}  \mathrm{M}_g  \\
    & + \alpha  d^{-1} R_{syl}   \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} L_{v_f}  \mathrm{M}_f + \beta d^{-1} R_{syl}   \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} L_{v_f}  \mathrm{M}_g
\end{align*}
where in the fourth inequality we have used the bound \eqref{eq:alpha-X-bound}, now we target the almost telescoping term. We set $v_f(x_{s},y_{s},\xi_{{s}+1})= v_s$, $v_f(x_{s+1}, y_{s+1}, \xi_{s+2}) = v_{s+1}$, $J_{yy}^{\alpha, \beta} = \Delta -  J_{yx}(U-U_0) $ and $B_{\alpha,\beta} = I - \beta J_{yy}^{\alpha, \beta}$. Since we are assuming 
\begin{align}
\beta < {1 \over \left \| \Delta \right\|_{\text{op}} +R_{ric} \left \| J_{yx}  \right\|_{\text{op}}} \label{eq: beta invertibility condition for slow bias}
\end{align}
then $B_{\alpha,\beta}$ is invertible using the Neumann series, we use the inverse to have:
\begin{align*}
    \sum_{s=0}^k B_{\alpha,\beta}^{k-s}X(v_s- v_{s+1}) =& \sum_{s=0}^k B_{\alpha,\beta}^{k-s}Xv_s- B_{\alpha,\beta} B_{\alpha,\beta}^{k-(s+1)}Xv_{s+1}\\
    =&\sum_{s=0}^k B_{\alpha,\beta}^{k-s}Xv_s- B_{\alpha,\beta}^{k-(s+1)}Xv_{s+1}+ \beta J_{yy}^{\alpha, \beta} B_{\alpha,\beta}^{-1} \sum_{s=0}^{k}  B_{\alpha,\beta}^{k-s}Xv_{s+1}\\
    = & B_{\alpha,\beta}^k X v_0 - B_{\alpha,\beta}^{-1}Xv_{k+1} + \beta J_{yy}^{\alpha, \beta} B_{\alpha,\beta}^{-1} \sum_{s=0}^{k}  B_{\alpha,\beta}^{k-s}Xv_{s+1}
\end{align*}
applying the norm: 
\begin{align*}
    \alpha \left \| \sum_{s=0}^k B_{\alpha,\beta}^{k-s}X(v_s- v_{s+1}) \right\| \leq & \alpha \left \| B_{\alpha,\beta}^k \right\|_{\text{op}} \left \|  X \right\|_{\text{op}} \left \|  v_0 \right\|  + \alpha \left \| B_{\alpha,\beta}^{-1} \right\|_{\text{op}} \left \| X \right\|_{\text{op}}\left \| v_{k+1} \right\| \\
    & + \alpha \beta \left \| J_{yy}^{\alpha, \beta} \right\|_{\text{op}} \left \|  B_{\alpha,\beta}^{-1} \right\|_{\text{op}} \left \| X \right\|_{\text{op}}\sum_{s=0}^{k}  \left \| B_{\alpha,\beta} \right\|_{\text{op}}^{k-s}\left \| v_{s+1} \right\|\\
    \leq &  \beta \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} R_{syl} e^{-d\beta k} \mathrm{M}_\xi + \beta \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} R_{syl}  \left \| B_{\alpha,\beta}^{-1} \right\|  \mathrm{M}_\xi \\
    & +  \beta^2 \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} R_{syl}  \left \| J_{yy}^{\alpha, \beta} \right\| \left \|  B_{\alpha,\beta}^{-1} \right\| \mathrm{M}_\xi \sum_{s=0}^{k}  e^{-d\beta (k-s)}\\
    \leq &  \beta R_{syl}  \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} \mathrm{M}_\xi \left (  e^{-d\beta k} +  \left \| B_{\alpha,\beta}^{-1} \right\| + \left \| J_{yy}^{\alpha, \beta} \right\| \left \|  B_{\alpha,\beta}^{-1} \right\| d^{-1}\right )  \\
    & \beta^2 R_{syl}  \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} \mathrm{M}_\xi \left \| J_{yy}^{\alpha, \beta} \right\| \left \|  B_{\alpha,\beta}^{-1} \right\| 
\end{align*}
and we get our bound for the first part, under our assumptions on the step sizes there is $C_A, C_J$ that satisfy $\left \|  B_{\alpha,\beta}^{-1} \right\| \leq C_B$ and  $\left \| J_{yy}^{\alpha, \beta} \right\|_{\text{op}} \leq C_J$ so we have: 
\begin{align*}
    \eqref{eq: poisson sum contraction cross}\leq & \beta \underbrace{R_{syl}  \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} \left ( d^{-1}(L_{v_f} + \mathrm{M}_{\mathcal S} \mathrm{M}_\xi L_{K} |\Xi|) \mathrm{M}_g  + \mathrm{M}_\xi \left (  1 +  C_B + \left \| J_{yy}^{\alpha, \beta} \right\| C_B d^{-1}\right ) \right ) }_{\triangleq C_1} \\
    & + \alpha  \underbrace{R_{syl}   \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} (L_{v_f} + \mathrm{M}_{\mathcal S} \mathrm{M}_\xi L_{K} |\Xi|)  \mathrm{M}_f d^{-1} }_{\triangleq C_2}+ \beta^2 \underbrace{ R_{syl}  \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} \left ( \mathrm{M}_\xi C_J C_B +  L_{v_f}  \mathrm{M}_g \right ) }_{\triangleq C_3}  \\
    & + \alpha \beta  \underbrace{ R_{syl}   \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} L_{v_f}  \mathrm{M}_f}_{\triangleq C_4}
\end{align*}
our last bound is for the term : 
\begin{align}
    \beta \left \| \sum_{j=0}^k  (I - \beta J_{yy}^{\alpha, \beta})^{k-j} I'\mathbb{E} \left [ \varphi_j \right ] \right\| \label{eq: poisson sum contraction slow}
\end{align}
from the Poisson decomposition the first term will equal zero under expectation. For the second term \eqref{eq: poisson slow 2 (bias)}, similarly to the previous part, we set $v_g(x_j,y_j, \xi_{j+2}) = v_j$ and $v_g(x_{j+1},y_{j+1}, \xi_{j+2}) = v_{j+1}$ we have:
\begin{align*}
    \beta \left \| \sum_{j=0}^k (I - \beta J_{yy}^{\alpha, \beta})^{k-j}  I'(v_j - v_{j+1}) \right\|
    \leq & \alpha \beta \left \| I' \right\|_{\text{op}} \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} L_{v_g}  \mathrm{M}_f + \beta d^{-1} \left \| I' \right\|_{\text{op}} \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} L_{v_g}  \mathrm{M}_g \\
    & + \alpha  d^{-1}\left \| I' \right\|_{\text{op}} \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} L_{v_g}  \mathrm{M}_f + \beta^2 \left \| I' \right\|_{\text{op}}\sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} L_{v_g}  \mathrm{M}_g 
\end{align*}
and the last term, we set $v_j = v_g(x_{j},y_{j},\xi_{{j}+1}), v_{j+1} = v_g(x_{j+1}, y_{j+1},\xi_{j+2})$:
\begin{align*}
    \beta \left \| \sum_{s=0}^k B_{\alpha,\beta}^{k-s}I'(v_s- v_{s+1}) \right\| \leq &  \beta \left \| I' \right\|_{\text{op}} \mathrm{M}_\xi \left (  \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} e^{-d\beta k} +  \left \| B_{\alpha,\beta}^{-1} \right\|_{\text{op}} + \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)}\left \| J_{yy}^{\alpha, \beta} \right\|_{\text{op}} \left \|  B_{\alpha,\beta}^{-1} \right\|_{\text{op}} d^{-1}  \right ) \\
    & +  \beta^2 \left \| I' \right\|_{\text{op}} \mathrm{M}_\xi \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)}\left \| J_{yy}^{\alpha, \beta} \right\|_{\text{op}} \left \|  B_{\alpha,\beta}^{-1} \right\|_{\text{op}}
\end{align*}
which implies the bound on the term: 
\begin{align*}
    \eqref{eq: poisson sum contraction slow} \leq &  \beta \underbrace{ \left \| I' \right\|_{\text{op}} \mathrm{M}_\xi \left (  C_B + \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} \left (  1 + d^{-1} \left ( C_J C_B +\left \| I' \right\|_{\text{op}} (L_{v_g} + \mathrm{M}_{\mathcal S} \mathrm{M}_\xi L_{K} |\Xi|) \mathrm{M}_g \right ) \right ) \right ) }_{\triangleq D_1}\\
    & +  \beta^2 \underbrace{\left ( \left \| I' \right\|_{\text{op}} \mathrm{M}_\xi \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} C_J C_B+\left \| I' \right\|_{\text{op}}\sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} L_{v_g}  \mathrm{M}_g \right ) }_{\triangleq D_2}\\
    & + \alpha \beta  \underbrace{\left \| I' \right\|_{\text{op}} \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} L_{v_g}  \mathrm{M}_f}_{\triangleq D_3}  + \alpha \underbrace{ d^{-1}\left \| I' \right\|_{\text{op}} \sqrt{\kappa(\mathrm G)} (L_{v_g} + \mathrm{M}_{\mathcal S} \mathrm{M}_\xi L_{K} |\Xi|)  \mathrm{M}_f }_{\triangleq D_4}
\end{align*}
which implies the following bound: 
\begin{align*}
     \eqref{eq: poisson sum contraction cross} + \eqref{eq: poisson sum contraction slow} \leq \alpha (C_2 + D_4) + \beta (C_1 + D_1 )+ \alpha \beta (C_4 + D_3) + \beta^2 (C_3 + D_2) 
\end{align*}
which concludes the proof.
<!-- end proof-11 -->
<!-- lemma-8: body_md -->
\label{lemma : Exp Sum Of the MSEs} Taking the sequence $\{x_k,y_k\}_{k\geq 0}$ defined by \eqref{eq: TTSA update} and under assumptions A.\ref{assumption: Boundedness}-\ref{assumption: Lip and Mono of g}, let $q,\gamma$ be positive real numbers, we have: 
\begin{align*}
    \sum_{s=0}^k e^{-q\gamma(k-s)} \mathbb{E} \left [  \left \| \hat y_{s} \right\|^2+\left \| \hat x_{s} \right\|^2 \right ]  \leq\left ( 1 + {1\over q\gamma}\right )  \left ( \mathrm{MSE}^{x}_\infty(\alpha, \beta) + \mathrm{MSE}^{y}_\infty(\alpha, \beta)\right ) + u_k^{q,\gamma}+w_k^{q,\gamma}
\end{align*}
<!-- end lemma-8 -->
<!-- proof-12: body_md -->
First, we start by analyzing the fast iterate, we invoke the result of Theorem \ref{thm : mse} to bound its MSE, we start by the sum of MSEs of the fast iterate:
\begin{align}
\sum_{s=0}^k e^{-q\gamma(k-s)}  \mathbb{E} \left [ \left \| \hat x_{s} \right\|^2 \right ]  \leq   & \sum_{s=0}^{k}e^{-q\gamma(k-s)}  \left ( (1-\alpha \mu_f)^{s}\left \| \hat x_{0} \right\|^2 + \mathrm{MSE}^{x}_\infty(\alpha, \beta)  \right ) \nonumber \\
  \leq & \sum_{s=0}^{k}e^{-q\gamma(k-s)}  \left ( (1-\alpha \mu_f)^{s} \left \| \hat x_{0} \right\|^2\right ) +  \mathrm{MSE}^{x}_\infty(\alpha, \beta)   \left ( 1 +\int_0^\infty e^{-q\gamma t}dt\right )  \nonumber \\
  = & \sum_{s=0}^{k}e^{-q\gamma(k-s)} \left ( (1-\alpha \mu_f)^{s} \left \| \hat x_{0} \right\|^2\right ) +  \mathrm{MSE}^{x}_\infty(\alpha, \beta)  \left ( 1+{1 \over q\gamma }\right )  \nonumber \\
 \leq &  \left \| \hat x_{0} \right\|^2 e^{-kq\gamma}\sum_{s=0}^{k}\left ( e^{q\gamma}(1-\alpha \mu_f)\right ) ^{s} \label{eq:sum-mse-fast-1}\\
& +  \left ( 1+{1 \over q\gamma }\right ) \mathrm{MSE}^{x}_\infty(\alpha, \beta) \nonumber
\end{align}
Now from our assumptions we have $ 1- \alpha\mu_f \in (0,1] $. If $e^{q\gamma}(1-\alpha \mu_f) = 1 $ then the sum equals $e^{-kq\gamma }\sum_{s=0}^{k}(e^{q\gamma}(1-\alpha \mu_f))^{s} =e^{-kq\gamma }(k+1)$ which will go to 0 as $k\to\infty$.  For the other case $e^{q\gamma}(1-\alpha \mu_f) \neq 1$ so the sum : 
\[
e^{-kq\gamma }\sum_{s=0}^{k}(e^{q\gamma}(1-\alpha \mu_f))^{s} = {e^{-kq\gamma} - e^{q\gamma} (1-\alpha \mu_f)^{k+1} \over 1 - e^{q\gamma} (1-\alpha \mu_f)}
\]
which will also disappear as k goes to infinity. So we set the sequence $u_k^{q,\gamma}$ to be defined by:
\[
u_k^{q,\gamma} = \left \| \hat x_{0} \right\|^2 e^{-kq\gamma }\sum_{s=0}^{k}\left ( e^{q\gamma}(1-\alpha \mu_f)\right ) ^{s}
\]
such that 
\[
\lim_{k\rightarrow \infty} u_k^{q,\gamma} = 0
\]
Same reasoning goes with the MSE of the slow iterate, from Theorem \ref{thm : mse} following the same steps in the fast MSE above we get:
\begin{align}
\sum_{s=0}^k e^{-q\gamma(k-s)} \mathbb{E} \left [ \left \| \hat y_{s} \right\|^2 \right ]  & \leq  \sum_{s=0 }^k e^{-q \gamma (k-s)} \left ( 1 - \beta \mu_g\right ) ^s \left \| \hat y_{0} \right\|^2 \nonumber\\
& + \sum_{s=0 }^k e^{-q \gamma (k-s)} D_1 \beta s (1 - \alpha \mu_f \wedge \beta \mu_g )^s\left \| \hat x_{0} \right\|^2\label{eq:sum-mse-slow-2}\\
& + \sum_{s=0 }^k e^{-q \gamma (k-s)} \mathrm{MSE}^{y}_\infty(\alpha, \beta) \label{eq:sum-mse-slow-3}
\end{align}
The first term follows the same reasoning as the term \eqref{eq:sum-mse-fast-1}, we focus on the second term \eqref{eq:sum-mse-slow-2}: 
\begin{align*}
    \eqref{eq:sum-mse-slow-2} \leq & \left \| \hat x_{0} \right\|^2 D_1 \beta ke^{-q \gamma k}  \sum_{s=0 }^k \left (  e^{q \gamma}  (1 - \alpha \mu_f \wedge \beta \mu_g )\right ) ^s
\end{align*}
if $e^{q \gamma}  (1 - \alpha \mu_f \wedge \beta \mu_g ) =1$:
\begin{align*}
    ke^{-q \gamma k}  \sum_{s=0 }^k \left (  e^{q \gamma}  (1 - \alpha \mu_f \wedge \beta \mu_g )\right ) ^s = e^{-q\gamma k} k(k+1)
\end{align*}
which vanishes as $k\to \infty$.

otherwise, if $e^{q \gamma}  (1 - \alpha \mu_f \wedge \beta \mu_g ) \neq1$:
\begin{align*}
    ke^{-q \gamma k}  \sum_{s=0 }^k \left (  e^{q \gamma}  (1 - \alpha \mu_f \wedge \beta \mu_g )\right ) ^s = {k e^{-q \gamma k} - k e^{q\gamma} (1 - \beta \mu_g \wedge \alpha \mu_f)^{k+1}\over 1 -e^{q\gamma} (1 - \beta \mu_g \wedge \alpha \mu_f) }
\end{align*}
which also vanishes as $k\to \infty$. We define the new sequence  $w_k^{q,\gamma}$ as:
\[
 w_k^{q,\gamma}=\sum_{s=0 }^k e^{-q \gamma (k-s)} \left ( 1 - \beta \mu_g\right ) ^s \left \| \hat y_{0} \right\|^2 + \sum_{s=0 }^k e^{-q \gamma (k-s)} D_1 \beta s (1 - \alpha \mu_f \wedge \beta \mu_g )^s\left \| \hat x_{0} \right\|^2
\]
such that 
\[
\lim_{k\rightarrow \infty} w_k^{q,\gamma}= 0
\]
and the last term \eqref{eq:sum-mse-slow-3} is bounded by 
\begin{align*}
    \eqref{eq:sum-mse-slow-3} \leq \left ( 1 + {1\over q\gamma}\right )  \mathrm{MSE}^{y}_\infty(\alpha, \beta)
\end{align*}
now we can write the final bound:
\begin{align*}
    \sum_{s=0}^k e^{-q\gamma(k-s)} \mathbb{E} \left [  \left \| \hat y_{s} \right\|^2+\left \| \hat x_{s} \right\|^2 \right ]  \leq \left ( 1 + {1\over q\gamma}\right )  \left ( \mathrm{MSE}^{x}_\infty(\alpha, \beta) + \mathrm{MSE}^{y}_\infty(\alpha, \beta)\right ) + u_k^{q,\gamma}+w_k^{q,\gamma}
\end{align*}
<!-- end proof-12 -->
